## Review Triage Log — settled source refresh, 2026-10-18

All three configured context-free layers reviewed frozen **272,148-byte** input `3ee6bb2cc2f40617eef37ef1c1e49092ebce484255fa7bdd5c99600abe118e0b`: the current two-document preparation/owner-continuation delta plus externally authored Platform compiled/test, Builds catalog/workflow and EventStore source deltas since the prior preparation. Historical evidence archives and unrelated prose were excluded. Blind floor is `min(floor(sqrt(272.148)+1),10)=10`. All 10 blind, six edge, one verification-gap and one Other finding have separate dispositions below. No source/test file was edited by this continuation. The owner restriction preserves original in-progress frontmatter/baseline/review-loop and records follow-ups only in these two existing documents.

| Finding | Verdict | Route | Verified evidence / disposition |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| RF-BH1 | medium | defer-existing-Agents-host | Actual Agents.UI uses Microsoft.NET.Sdk.Razor and has no executable entry point; AddProject treats it as a runnable host. The synthetic Web fixture does not establish real UI launch. Platform owner must supply the real receiving web host before any positive Agents-host claim. |
| RF-BH2 | medium | defer-existing-Agents-infrastructure | AgentsHostComposition adds projects without sidecars/state/invocation/domain-registration wiring, while AgentDomainHostComposition requires Dapr and leaves several adapters deferred. An accepted optional profile is insufficient for working Agents readiness. |
| RF-BH3 | medium | defer-existing-Agents-bindings | The host emits Agents__Parties__Binding and Agents__Conversations__Binding, while the actual adapters select Parties:BaseUrl/Tenant and Agents:EventStore:BaseUrl. Those bindings do not activate the supported live clients/dispatcher. |
| RF-BH4 | medium | defer-existing-Agents-admission | TryCompose supplies null capabilities; the nonnull overload registers private services in the AppHost only. AdmitAsync has no receiving-process production caller. This does not establish a functioning cross-process replay/spool admission boundary. |
| RF-BH5 | medium | defer-existing-Agents-bootstrap | Tenants BootstrapHostedService constructs BootstrapGlobalAdmin with the configured literal UserId. Passing a reference label through Tenants__BootstrapGlobalAdminUserId does not resolve it to the intended administrator. |
| RF-BH6 | medium | defer-existing-authority-contract | QueryCallerAuthorityBinding emits only its digest; WorkloadAuthorizationDetails.Decode requires tenant/domain for every nonempty binding set, and AcquireBoundAssertionAsync invokes it before transport. Asymmetric caller-query issuance therefore refuses that producer output. |
| RF-BH7 | maybe-false | defer-unverified-authority-policy | Exact static grants do require every requested digest value to be enrolled. Whether generic fresh-query signing is an intended supported authority capability is unresolved: the issuer explicitly promises exact-binding grants, and no approved dynamic-digest authorization policy is present. EventStore authority owner must settle that policy before claiming general fresh-query support; do not weaken the grant check. |
| RF-BH8 | medium | defer-existing-recovery-integrity | For an original pending Match(ETag) envelope without a terminal anchor, recovery validates prior-value presence but not an independent digest. CompensateAsync restores PreviousBytes; its later anchor hashes the already-read bytes, so a different valid previous JSON value can be restored after a conflict. Reopen with persisted-value corruption evidence before stronger recovery-integrity claims. |
| RF-BH9 | medium | defer-existing-query-page-mapping | DaprDomainQueryInvoker preserves only InvalidEnvelope/InvalidCursor for HTTP400. A supported InvalidPage result is converted to ActorException, losing client-error classification; share the same follow-up as RF-EH5. |
| RF-BH10 | medium | defer-existing-profile-reference-validation | OptionalReference checks size/whitespace and a credential-substring blacklist, without requiring a reference scheme. Compact raw credential-shaped strings pass and are forwarded to project environment. The optional Agents profile owner must define and validate supported reference schemes before asserting secret exclusion. |
| RF-EH1 | medium | defer-existing-authority-contract | Independently traced the same digest-only producer to mandatory tenant/domain decode and bound issuance refusal. This row retains the separate edge finding; action shares RF-BH6. |
| RF-EH2 | medium | defer-existing-authority-body-deadline | ResponseHeadersRead finishes HttpClient send-time coverage at headers; ReadBoundedTokenResponseAsync streams with caller cancellation only. A stalled successful body can hold issuance and the ordinary shared gate indefinitely when the caller supplies no deadline. This is the HTTP authority branch, not local HMAC signing. |
| RF-EH3 | medium | defer-existing-authority-key-refresh | The bound branch validates against GetAuthorityMetadataAsync cached for five minutes and returns null on validation failure without a key-not-found refresh. Newly rotated signing keys can therefore deny fresh authority assertions until refresh. |
| RF-EH4 | maybe-false | defer-unverified-principal-provenance | Capture enumerates every identity role type and principal claim once the first identity is authenticated; a constructed mixed principal will include unauthenticated-identity claims. Actual untrusted claim provenance through a supported gateway authentication/transformation producer is not demonstrated. Need that producer/host trace and its trust contract before asserting privilege escalation; selected local state reads do not call Capture. |
| RF-EH5 | medium | defer-existing-query-page-mapping | Checked the separate edge trigger: HTTP400 carrying InvalidPage is replaced by ActorException in DaprDomainQueryInvoker. Same underlying mapping omission as RF-BH9; no pagination transport success is claimed. |
| RF-EH6 | medium | defer-existing-delegated-response-metadata | DelegatedOwnerQueryClient calls the two-argument FromPayload with query.QueryType as projectionType; that overload discards SubmitQueryResponse.Metadata. Owner query/projection types may differ, and freshness/paging metadata is lost. This client is absent from the retention consumer path. |
| RF-VG1 | low | defer-existing-query-denial-regression | Accepted the reviewer-preverified coverage gap: existing invoker/router/submission tests do not compose HTTP403/404 or body safe-denial through canonical QueryNotFoundException. Current router source contains the correct safe-denial branch; the gap is regression protection, not a demonstrated failed current result. Reopen before a stronger composed-denial verification claim. |
| RF-VO1 | medium | defer-existing-authority-contract | Verified the verification layer Other finding independently: opt-in/client credentials cannot overcome digest-only authorization_details being rejected before the asymmetric authority call. Separate row retained; shared action with RF-BH6 and RF-EH1. |

These pre-existing capability follow-ups remain with **Jérôme Piquot / Platform composition owner** (Agents) and **Jérôme Piquot / EventStore maintainers** (query authority, transport, delegation and recovery). The two maybe-false findings are explicitly unverified potential medium issues; the stated host/policy evidence is required to settle them. Reopen before enabling or making positive claims about the affected capability, signing profile, credential exclusion, pagination/delegation, recovery integrity or composed-denial regression coverage. No wildcard authorization, source repair or declaration change is introduced. The deferred-work ledger stays unchanged under the owner two-document restriction.

No finding establishes a defect in the selected local retention operation. Builds calls **PlatformAppModel.ComposeRun**, separately from the optional Agents ComposeEntry branch. The retention consumer posts only its fixed local workload and reads Dapr strong state/actor control and the fixed PostgreSQL query; it does not use handler/paged/delegated caller-query routes, authority HTTP issuance or pending-batch recovery. ComposeRun supplies the local Development signing contract; JwtWorkloadAssertionIssuer chooses its signing-key branch before asymmetric resource-binding handling. The 29-case graph evidence and five Identity cases retain their stated offline scope; neither claims working Agents deployment, production query authority or full dependency closure. If prerequisites are unresolved or the proposed request time is missed, keep the plan inactive and prepare a fresh absolute window; never shift or extend an existing run.

With cwd `/home/administrator/projects/hexalith/platform`, and only after the supported local tool pin and isolated verified Dapr home have exact resolved identities, the proposed invocation is:

```bash
dotnet tool run hexalith-module test --manifest /home/administrator/projects/hexalith/platform/references/Hexalith.Memories/module/hexalith.module-manifest.v2.json --profile functional-retention --mode source --output json
```

`HEXALITH_DAPR_HOME` must name the actual owner-verified isolated home, not an invented path. No restore, install, fetch, dependency update, checkout, gitlink change or fallback is authorized now. The final usable approval must explicitly bind any normal startup cache hydration or pinned-image retrieval to the resolved declared versions/digests and original startup budget; otherwise missing artifacts refuse the operation. No dependency selection may change. The runner invokes the declared Python entry with its existing selected environment and fixed native TRX destination; no arbitrary commands, SQL, URL or infrastructure authority are given to the Memories verifier.

Expected declaration closure is Memories plus EventStore: four source services (EventStore gateway and Memories server/clock/telemetry), their four Dapr CLI/native sidecars, the existing finite EventStore startup seed task if declared, and the runner-owned AppHost/DCP. Expected seven containers are Redis, PostgreSQL, Memories vector/graph storage and Dapr sentry/placement/scheduler. The actual persisted plan/resource observations must match that closure; an unexpected resource or unresolved ownership refuses readiness. Ports, process start identities and run/container labels come from the actual runner allocation. No Tenants, Kubernetes, OpenBao, VM, SSH or external target is included.

PostgreSQL ceilings are 8 CPU, 16 GiB memory with the same swap ceiling, 100 connections, a 40-connection Dapr pool and 400 GiB tmpfs maximum. This maximum is neither a reservation nor a measured demand. Host capacity/headroom and total graph capacity remain unobserved and must be checked before startup in the later authorized operation. Storage is ephemeral run-owned tmpfs; no retained disk is formatted or resized. Private TLS keys, observer password file, local file secrets, gate and scheduler state stay within the existing private run workspace. Development UTC and Debug source builds are disclosed local limitations.

One initial request owns mapping/build/startup within 14,400 seconds, with the existing 600-second startup cap and separate 900-second stopping cap. Fresh readiness must be at most 60 seconds old and leave at least 4,501 seconds to execute. Startup includes the actual registered CRUD/ETag/transaction/actor-metadata/TTL/bounds/authority probes; offline doubles provide no runtime probe credit. One writer submits 125 records for A, waits at least 1,800 actual UTC and monotonic seconds, then submits 125 for B. Before any cohort, the exact fixed read-only SQL must execute successfully against the actual Dapr-created schema and return no A rows. After B, capture all actual A payload/TTL bounds at A+3,540 seconds, then strongly read A at A+3,590 seconds with at most sixteen concurrent existing GETs in fixed key order. Observe A expiry/absence and reminder/index progress by the existing +900-second grace, and verify all canonical B records unchanged throughout. A SQL/schema error refuses the operation before cohort submission; offline doubles do not satisfy this preflight. PostgreSQL observations use only the fixed read-only observer/TCP/verify-full queries. No simulated time or record backdating is allowed in that operation.

Each cohort has at most two submissions total, with the second permitted only for uncertain transport using the same identity and timestamp. No automatic infrastructure restart, new run, cleanup retry, cohort-clock reset or deadline extension is permitted. Drift, invalid/expired gate, withdrawal, incorrect observations, semantic failure or budget exhaustion stops native work and closes the gate. Success requires actual owned cleanup. A failed local run keeps the existing FR-4 retained-resource policy, with new local workload, purge and heartbeat mutations refused, the first failed outcome preserved, and actual retained/remaining/unknown disposition recorded. Already-admitted SDK operations may finish; subsequent mutation boundaries refuse closure. A failed one-shot capability attempt may leave its finite probe keys in that retained backend as diagnostic failure state; this earns no cleanup credit and cannot trigger more probe attempts. Withdrawal follows the runner's bounded automatic cleanup. Gate-closure failure or unverified quiescence/cleanup remains a failure; no pass or continuing workload authority is inferred.

Do not append an automatic `down` invocation to a retained failure: current `DownAsync(string, ...)` starts a fresh selected 900-second budget, so doing that would reset the approved stopping budget. Retained infrastructure disposal requires its own later concrete owner action against the actual returned run identity; it grants no workload restart. Retain the native TRX, detailed observation artifact/hash, exact source/runtime/backend/readiness identities, first terminal outcome and actual cleanup disposition through the existing result/evidence paths. No new report protocol or functional-to-full predecessor conversion is introduced.

## Intent

Deliver the smallest real PostgreSQL retention experiment through Hexalith.Platform's supported local run-owned environment. Platform owns infrastructure and lifecycle; Memories consumes its existing runner descriptor/result and verifies behavior. One existing story, verifier and consolidated report; no new framework, AppHost, runner, receipt protocol or numbered specification.

## Boundaries & Constraints

Implementation/offline preparation only: no workload, VM, container, AppHost, Dapr, cluster or server start/contact; no storage allocation/format, credentials/active permission change, dependency init/update, Git mutation, A41/sprint transition or Production. No server contact at 192.168.1.30; hosted attachment is refused by Platform FR4. Standing owner policy is already approved. Preserve previous closed and unused VM budget/decision bytes without consuming, withdrawing or transferring them.

### Functional preparation implementation

The existing CLI now selects the private fixed orchestration, target and guest helpers. They bind the source/input/tool bytes, real SDK-produced OCI archives, an immutable host-only asset catalog, corrected QEMU frontend/backend syntax, new inactive expiring SSH seed, fresh process/UUID/guest/context identity, an exclusive consumed-once execution claim, scoped gate/Lease changes and measured terminal API readback before owned guest stop. Current owner authority is re-read before actions; long streams check fresh controls and input metadata. A source re-render after operational admission prevents prepared manifest substitution. Inputs cannot supply executable, shell, SQL or destination choices.

The functional render deliberately differs from the full approved profile: K3s `v1.35.9+k3s1`; static local Retain PVs instead of OpenEBS provisioner qualification; one 400-GiB PostgreSQL claim, Redis 20 GiB, FalkorDB 10 GiB, file-backed TLS OpenBao 60 GiB, and three 17-GiB Dapr Scheduler claims (541 GiB declared total within the retained 600-GiB disk). These are requests/reservations, not hard filesystem quotas or physical free-space proof. Dapr 1.18.1 and PostgreSQL 18.6 immutable image pins, PostgreSQL SCRAM/verify-full TLS, scoped OpenBao secrets, real startup capability probes and signed clock validation remain. The OpenBao chart is retained as a pinned input; this functional setup uses the existing disposable service pattern rather than claiming chart/HA/platform acceptance. No exact-profile, at-rest, capacity, backup/restore, allocator, fault or Production credit follows from this setup.

The collector retains strong record/hash/expiry observations, exact expiry-bucket reads and durable actor reminder/purge progress. TTL attribution requires strong absence while the exact expired PostgreSQL tuple is still physically present. A disappeared tuple alone cannot establish actor deletion; unresolved per-record attribution cannot pass. Read-only corroboration never inserts/deletes records or drives purge.

Local source Debug OCI builds succeeded with SDK `10.0.401`, the pinned ASP.NET base `eca1355b1fefb0a93617b7d01749dc8ac821e0eadcedbcf7d64e4b59cad60104`, and recorded commands/output hashes. ## Review Triage Log

Three configured reviewers examined the preparation delta from `61074e981bd92019488db19da0c8572bd4bf8b97`; the parent remains in-progress because live/full checkpoints are excluded from this preparation approval. Each finding is recorded individually before grouping. These are local corrections within the approved intent; no frozen requirement is reduced.

| Finding | Verdict | Route | Verified evidence / correction |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| BH1 | medium | patch | New certificate/CSR/serial files are created after the chmod pass; private inventory rejects mode0644. Normalize the new bundle files after rendering. |
| BH2 | high | patch | Debug source references and imported root build inputs exist outside source_files; the current provenance omits demonstrated build inputs. Include permitted resolved dependencies and rebuild provenance. |
| BH3 | high | patch | Waits use UTC while monotonic is only an upper bound; a forward step can satisfy the lower retention bound early. Require elapsed monotonic intervals and reject clock discontinuity. |
| BH4 | medium | patch | startup_probes performs three bounded mutations/rollouts yet is classified as a 120-second read. Remove it from retryable read classification; retain setup deadline. |
| BH5 | medium | patch | That same read classification replays scaling after transport uncertainty. Stop/reconcile restoration instead of retrying the mutating startup method. |
| BH6 | high | patch | Guest fileDigests omit the downloaded executable/chart/airgap and third-party OCI content. Verify fixed download hashes and complete OCI content linkage at consumption. |
| BH7 | high | patch | Long setup children check authority/input drift but not resource floors. Add bounded monitoring during the already admitted operation. |
| BH8 | medium | patch | The caller dereferences missing transport after concrete missing-tool blockers, losing the blocker. Return a structured blocked preparation result. |
| BH9 | high | patch | OpenBao unseal key appears in Kubernetes exec arguments. Send the unseal request payload through protected stdin. |
| BH10 | medium | patch | Guest node wait retries all RuntimeError values for 300 seconds. Replace with an explicitly bounded readiness watch; do not retry semantic failures. |
| EC1 | high | patch | QMP quit is outside its handler; failure bypasses verified PID termination. Always reach identity-checked TERM/KILL fallback. |
| EC2 | high | patch | Retained disk identities are checked only before potentially long staging. Revalidate immediately before QEMU spawn. |
| EC3 | high | patch | Same demonstrated unchecked guest-consumption path as BH6; downloaded transport bytes need final consumption checks. |
| EC4 | medium | patch | Same startup read classification as BH4/BH5 causes both false 120-second refusal and mutation replay. |
| EC5 | high | patch | Gate disable and scaling verify objectUID but not the current Lease holder. Recheck ownership before every restoration mutation, including after release. |
| EC6 | high | patch | Preparation copies archives before aggregate capacity/free-space checks. Enforce the existing limits before and throughout writes. |
| EC7 | high | patch | Same demonstrated missing reference/build closure as BH2; Directory.Packages.props imports Hexalith.Builds input outside the current map. |
| VG1 | medium | patch | The filed searches establish no CI discovery of the new suite. Register it in the existing CI path with its local render prerequisites. |
| VG2 | medium | patch | The filed tests invoke guards directly and cannot detect disconnected BeforeTargets hooks. Add harmless publish/pack entry-target marker fixtures. |
| VG3 | high | patch | The filed searches establish no execution test of the formatting safeguard. Add sandboxed disk-identity/blankness/single-attempt tests without real devices. |
| VG4 | medium | patch | Authority tests use real datetime.now against a fixed 2026 window. Pin the fixture clock, preserving explicit expiry-negative cases. |

## Historical Spec 14 notes — retained verbatim below

The following is the pre-correction body, retained for history. It is not current execution guidance and cannot reinstate D0, old approval ceremonies or old competing plans.


<frozen-after-approval reason="human-owned intent — do not modify unless human renegotiates">

## Intent

**Problem:** Story 27.4 needs an owner-approved bounded run, complete PG2 C1 predecessor, live C2–C6 evidence and A41 publication proof.

**Approach:** Use the adopted sole-owner policy, prepare one bounded run record for owner approval, collect and verify C1 and qualification evidence, then record owner acceptance and verify A41 publication.

## Boundaries & Constraints

**Always:** Bind authority and evidence to approved `PG-ONPREM-2` SHA-256 `95aa36e8aba8d9914566f6df894c33098739251040dd0cc4b2917f76a994ccdb` and workload SHA-256 `71903bb8cc1889a015e066b0276fba2c7f073b2bdfc4d3b11225fc79ec6f091f`. Check current owner approval, expiry and withdrawal. Require 25 verified C1 gates in one eligible session, explicit fault/purge scopes, distinct owner-accepted C5/C6 records, and disabled gate, empty Lease and zero lifecycle/clock replicas at finish. Use private owner-controlled hashed/readback evidence for non-Production qualification; separate receipt services, independent human reviewers and external immutable custody are not required for this mode.

**Never:** Treat policy adoption, historical PG1 C1.15, closed-window C1.16, fixtures or role labels as a live run approval. Do not claim independent human review for the sole owner’s decisions. Contact an unapproved target, enable Production writes, weaken predecessor refusal, expose secrets, change the zero-replica overlay, or resolve A41 and its sprint action before publication proof.

## I/O & Edge-Case Matrix

| Scenario | Input / State | Expected Outcome | Failure Behavior |
|----------|---------------|------------------|------------------|
| Hold | Missing, expired, changed or withdrawn owner run approval or C1 | `RECHECK_ONLY`; no target or A41 mutation | Name the missing fact |
| Qualification | Current owner run approval, verified C1, scoped C2–C4 actions | Hash-bound packets prove acknowledgement, retention, faults, audit continuity and privacy denial | Reject partial proof and restore disabled/empty/zero |
| Close-out | Owner-accepted C0–C6, terminal proof and publication approval | Exact A41 transition, protected bytes intact, remote containment proven | Reject drift or missing approval |

</frozen-after-approval>
